# Queue
- **`symbols --editor` and the content mapper write some valid Contract shape names as invalid TypeScript** (2026-11-14, the third reviews of #406 and #397). A shape named with a hyphen (`A-B`, which `tests/it/rust_shapes.rs` covers) is written verbatim into `"type"` and into signatures (`contract/cli/src/symbols/types.rs`, `editors/content-mapper/names.js`), and one named with a TypeScript reserved word (`shape class`) becomes `type class = …`. Give such shapes safe, collision-free TypeScript aliases in both places and test hyphenated, reserved and colliding names. Separately, the mapper keys a symbol by its declaration's line and column but reads an imported file from disk, so an unsaved edit above `Row` in `row.contract` disconnects its importer's references and a rename can miss the `use`; test it with `didChange` on the imported file.
- **A web menu's rows have no `menuitem` role** (2026-11-14, found reviewing #451). A `button` or `a[href]` in a `role="menu"` popover keeps its own role on the web host, as `docs/recipes/list-rows.md` writes it, so a screen reader meets a menu with no menu items; `input-glue.js` gives those rows the menu's arrow keys but no role. The host should give each such row `menuitem` (WAI-ARIA's menu requires its items to have it), as NSMenu and UIMenu items are items, rather than every author writing `role="menuitem"`.
- **The painted hosts never focus a plain link in a line of text** (2026-11-14, found landing #404). The presenter's `focusable` reads the `link` and `button` roles, not `href`, so an inline `a[href]` with no `press` or `tabindex` is not focusable on Linux, Windows or Android's reader, and a menu's or tablist's arrows pass over it there, where the web and the Mac reach it (LLP 1166.000 §8).
- **The deploy receipt-reuse test cannot read rustc 1.100's unit dependency information** (2026-11-14, also reproduced with origin/main's deploy code). `identical deploy bakes reuse compilation and retain each run receipt` in `scripts/deploy-paths.test.mjs` switches its fixture from the pinned compiler to the installed `stable` toolchain, then `unitDepInfo` refuses `receipts-linux` at the `run-compiler` build. Check the dependency-information format that toolchain writes and keep the receipt's source closure intact.
- **Deploy counts an external app's generated workspace and editor files as source changes** (2026-11-14, reproducing #555 with `exact new`). `sourcePathspec` excludes `.shells`, but the generated workspace lives at `.exact/shells`; `app.contract.d.ts` and the feedback log and diary also enter the capture. A clean app refuses without `--dirty` after its first build. Keep generated outputs out of the source inventory while preserving authored inputs.
- **A secret write leaves the Rust launch-preload test past its deadline** (2026-11-14, macOS 27.0.1, Rust 1.97.0, base `9d887aa33`). Six `launch_preloads::tests::rust_launch` tests time out in the default Cargo suite. A serial run of that group passes 32 and still fails `a_function_secret_write_drops_a_request_built_with_the_old_value`: the launch records `LaunchDeadline` at 5,010 ms. Found while qualifying #549, which changes no Rust code.
- **ArrowDown leaves a plain menu button focused under the Mac agent** (2026-11-14, #549's reduced picker on macOS 27.0.1). In a `popover="auto" role="menu"` of ordinary buttons, `type bot-Ada key ArrowDown` followed by `tree --ax` still marks Ada focused. Enter and Escape work after #549's fix; qualify arrow navigation for AppKit-backed menu rows separately.
- **The generated-game command test's recording SDK lacks node_modules** (2026-11-14, reproduced on the base of #524). `bun test game/new.test.mjs --test-name-pattern "generated commands run from an external author directory with quoted paths"` fails at line 122: the Windows command refuses its fake SDK before reaching its recording build script. Supply the fixture's build prerequisites while keeping host builds mocked.
- **Qualify SQLite on the minimum iOS in the async lane (#565).** Add an iOS 17 simulator launch that saves and reopens Fieldnotes data. Current-runtime launches do not catch imports absent from the minimum OS; no iOS 17 runtime is installed on the fixing lane's Mac.
- **A second Contract stack-depth case aborts on main** (2026-11-14, reproduced with the compiler at `9d887aa33`). `stack_depth::discarded_slot_fill_under_deep_callers_fits_a_small_stack` overflows its 2 MiB thread and aborts the integration binary. Run it alone until the overflow is fixed.
- **The Linux host Hermes has no `Intl.Collator`, so a Linux bake of a data module that uses it at top level fails** (2026-11-14, seen qualifying #205 on fleet-expo-build-1000). `typeof Intl.Collator` is `undefined` in the Linux bake while `DateTimeFormat` and `NumberFormat` exist, so `new Intl.Collator().compare(...)` at a module's top level stops the build; the Android v6 bundle and Apple have it. Give the Linux bundle Collator (the Android v6 build's ICU route) or say so in the reference. On the same emulator, `bun exact.mjs test android --headless` of that app passed its first mutation's checks and failed the second (count stayed 1), on an x86_64 emulator without KVM translating arm64; rerun on an arm64 emulator before treating it as a defect.
- **The Duo smoke's `lab` leg fails four checks on main** (2026-11-13, run at `32c22e0a` with and without a change to Duo Lab's Fold detail, on the iPhone Duo simulator, iOS 27.1, which reports a right inset of 84 points and a bottom inset of 34). Fully open and closed, Fold's panes leave out both insets: 867 of 951 points wide, ending at 635 of 669, and 382 of 466, ending at 644 of 678. Partially folded, the panes sit on the segments, but the list pane ends at 635 where the detail pane reaches 669. The Combinations title is centred in 867 points, not 951. At `b3996a11` these checks passed. Find the change that brought the insets in, then fix the app or the smoke.
- **A select's list on Windows is a MenuFlyout, not a ComboBox's** (2026-11-13). It opens as the top layer's Fluent menu under the select (`presenter/top/select.rs`), the chosen option checked. WinUI's `ComboBox` opens its list over the box with the selected item aligned to it, and marks that item with a filled row and an accent pill, not a check (LLP 1115.004 D10.4).
- **`TextPlaceIOSTests.testHalfWayThroughTheFadeTheScreenShowsBothLinesBlended` fails on main** (2026-11-13, iPhone simulator, at 221e551bf; seen landing q0563 and failing the same at its base). `bun host/apple/build.mjs --test --ios --only TextPlaceIOSTests` runs it.
- **The async lane's Mac still lacks what two of its checks need** (2026-11-13). On the Mac Studio that runs the first tier, its `vm-extract` step reports UNAVAILABLE: Charon and Aeneas are not installed (`semantics/vm-extract/README.md`, "Tools"). The second tier is not started: it needs the `aarch64-pc-windows-gnullvm` Rust target, the pinned `x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu` Hermes bundle, and `JAVA_HOME` set to Homebrew's OpenJDK (installed, not linked) in its environment. An issue the lane files is written into its root's working tree and is neither committed nor pushed: its notes reach origin, its issue files do not.

- The bake producer's HTTP-namespace test fails on main with Bun 1.4.2: `served_pair` in `js/bake/tests/producer.rs` runs `bun --input-type=module -e <probe> <out>`, which refuses `<out>` as a module. Reproduced with origin/main's producer and bake library.
- **A web menu opened by a click shows its first row highlighted** (2026-11-13, seen in `menu-checks.test.mjs`'s screenshots, Chrome). Since 4916c5289 a menu focuses its first usable row when it opens, and `host/web/neutral.css`'s menu `:focus` rule washes the focused row, so a click-opened menu opens with a highlight that NSMenu and UIMenu show only after the pointer moves or an arrow key is pressed. A tell under LLP 1115; the wash follows `:focus`, which the pointer also moves (`input-glue.js`), so the fix is to wash a row focused on open only when the menu was opened from the keyboard.
- **`contextmenu.test.mjs` fails "wasm popover and focus commands preserve authored order" on main** (2026-11-13, three runs of three on this Mac): `host/web/tests/contextmenu.test.mjs:162` expects `['first', 'trigger']` and gets `['', '']`.
- `GroupedListIOSTests.testATappedRowStaysSelectedThroughThePushAndLetsGoOnReturn` fails on an iPhone 17 Pro simulator with iOS 27.0: the first route push leaves one controller instead of two, both in the standalone run and in the hosted app with its navigation fixture. Seen on the #379 branch; origin/main was not checked.

- **Grouped section pointer hover on iPad (#379).** Forward hover from UIKit’s visible cells, header and footer to the logical section, clearing it on exit, reuse and removal. The section’s own hover recognizer is on its hidden authored view, so a real pointer cannot activate its shadow.
- **Photo Editor property suggestions miss a kept enum block.** `cargo test -p contract-difftest --lib suggest::precision_tests::the_nineteen_kept_blocks_survive_without_existing_property_suppression` fails to suggest "printSize is one of 3 values". It fails with both unshared control plans and executable sharing; the app-plan observations agree through the seeded drives.

- realworld (68.1 KiB brotli against 45), caltrain (50.0 KiB against 25) and video-player (29.3 KiB against 17) are over their web byte budgets on main at 69668888a (`bun scripts/metrics.mjs --web-bytes`).
- `exact-runner`'s incremental test (`every_app_plan_updates_incrementally_exactly_as_it_does_in_full`, the geofence fixture) fails on main with unchanged runner code (seen by three lanes on Linux).
- `HeaderMacTests.testASearchInputIsTheBarsSearchItemHoldingTheInputsOwnField` fails on main: the search item's field editor is nil (`HeaderMacTests.swift:943`).
- **The web module substitute ignores `deniedAnswers` and `whilePrompt`** (2026-11-13, read in the reviews of q0539; not run). `host/web-js/modules.js` honours a manifest's `checks`, `continues` and `facts` but not these two keys, so on the web asking for motion permission while it is denied answers an error rather than the status, and reading the accelerometer while permission is `prompt` returns a sample rather than an error (`packages/motion/exact.json`). Since a4a02c83f `modules.js` reads `deniedAnswers` for a held send; `whilePrompt` is still unread, and the motion case above has not been run again.
- **Quads and hooks still snap on any parent edit** (2026-11-13, read, not run). Particles, sprites and placed planes (`game/render/src/quads.rs` `feed_with_pose`) and the hook frame poses (`hooks.rs` `sync`) pass a world-wide parent revision to `scene::snap`, so any `Parent` edit cuts their interpolation for a tick. The scene, lights and sockets now ask `ParentChanges` for their own chain (`scene::snap_in`); give these the same.
- **Grouped section shadow keyframes (#379).** Project shadow tracks onto the card on every graphical host, with its base shadow supplying implicit endpoints; keep other animated properties on the section. Until then, the compiler refuses grouped section shadow keyframes; shadow transitions are supported.

- **`exact-js-web`'s browser test fails on main** (2026-11-14, run on this Mac). `browser::browser_modules_guard_their_own_builtins_and_refuse_bad_candidates`: the page's `exactBodyFrom` check (`js/web/tests/it/browser.rs:698`) reads kind 2 for both the typed and the picked body where it expects 0 (`exactBodyFrom on the page: 2 2 2`). The test is outside the gate, so nothing else reports it.
- **`exact-render`'s stream test fails on main** (2026-11-13, run on this Mac). `stream::a_stream_completes_the_render_at_its_first_message_and_is_listed_open` (`host/render/tests/it/stream.rs:91`) finds 94 messages parsed, where the render should complete at the first and let the stream go (29 to 1,125 in other runs). Fails alone and on a clean origin/main checkout.
- **A request can be served another build's bytes under a generation that left and came back** (2026-11-13, found in the reviews of the `generationFile` fix; not yet reproduced). `generationFile` and `servedFile`'s unpinned branch (`host/web/generations.mjs`) take a file as generation A's when `generationOf(dist)` names A both before and after the read. If the builds change from A to B and back to a byte-identical A meanwhile, the lookup can resolve B's tree (its `.previous` during the last swap's rename gap) and the read return B's bytes labelled A: a generation is a digest of the build's files, so equal generations do not say which tree the bytes came from. Check the identity of the tree read, its root's device and inode, across the lookup and the read, beside its generation.
- **UIKit cover boot clearance.** `CoverBootIOSTests.testTheCoverRootUsesTheWholeViewInItsFirstSettlement` passes its initial viewport checks but expects bottom clearance 0 where the route reports 50 on iPhone 17 Pro, iOS 27.0. Literal and child-derived `viewport-fit="cover"` fail identically. Reconcile the assertion with `NavigationCoversIOS.routeEdges`.

- **`host/web/tests/dev-scheduler.test.mjs` fails three cases on main** (2026-11-13): the generated-game-arguments, game-edit and declared-source-file cases throw `ReferenceError: basename is not defined` from the `changedPath` they slice out of `host/web/dev.mjs`; the test's sandbox does not provide `basename`.
- **The JS target asks a queued send as App** (2026-11-12, read, not run). `host/web-js/schedule.js` keeps a waiting send's source and arguments (`m.wait`) but not the origin of the commit that queued it, and its `next` commit runs under whatever origin is ambient, so a tool's or a wake's queued send, its request (now stamped with `Origin.now()`, `resources.js`) and its `then` are the app's. The runner keeps that origin, invocation and causes (`queue.rs` `Waiting`). Keep them on the JS target and restore them around the `next` commit.
- **Linux's neutral buttons: two web metrics left** (2026-11-12, after the symbol scales and image gap landed). A sidebar tab with a symbol and a title gets the web's `sidebar-gap` (10px) there, but Linux has no sidebar presentation of a tab list (it always draws a tab bar). A symbol-only button with an `aria-label` in a modal sheet's header paints its symbol on Linux where the web shows the accessible name as text ("Discard").
- **A native route can cover an authored root tablist without an explicit paint order** (2026-11-12, iPhone 17 Pro simulator, iOS 27.0). The `overflowing-tabs.contract` fixture without its tablist’s `z-index=1` lays the active route across the full viewport and the driver refuses the tab press because that route covers its middle. Check default authored-tab paint order; the cover-feedback regression gives its bar an explicit order.

- **A Mac list's fast fling still misses 120 Hz** (2026-11-12, #383; LLP 1078 §5.1). On a 5,120-row table list of two-paragraph rows, eight trackpad flings of 3,000 points through the window server, Instruments' Animation Hitches measure 56 to 270 ms of hitch a second of scrolling in the release build (115 to 515 before "A rebound Mac row writes only what changed of its paint and accessibility" and the two commits before it, in the same interleaved rounds at load averages 8 to 63). A hand-built `NSTableView` of the same rows measures 3 to 47 on this shared machine, so it, not "under 5", is the bar here. The main thread spends about 1.4 ms a rebound row (7.9 s over about 5,800 rows), the hand-built table about 0.5. In Time Profiler's main-thread samples over the flings (xctrace, which does not perturb the app as `sample` does), AppKit's display and commit of the layers are half (3,266 of 6,445): each rebound paragraph is drawn again in `draw` on the main thread (0.044 ms, making a typesetter for paint attributes the owner's measurement lacked, LLP 1072 #7), AppKit rebuilds its layer tree (6%) and tracking areas (6%) for rows moving between table cells, and `wantsUpdateLayer` asks `canRasterText` at every display (2%). The text pass is 11%; the table feed with AppKit's table layout, and the ops, are the rest. Tried and not kept: building ahead by how late the main thread ran (fewer rescues, more hitch), and asynchronous fills on the Mac (LLP 1072 stage 5: the frame harness's held-scroll frames about 30% cheaper, but hitch time 220 to 404 against 66 to 154, as a rescue lands the slice in flight inside AppKit's scroll synchronizer). Next: paint a rebound row's paragraphs from the owner's shape so `draw` makes no typesetter; keep a rebound row in the cell the table hands its new item instead of moving it; answer `wantsUpdateLayer` without `canRasterText`; a rescue that does not wait for a slice in flight, then asynchronous fills again. Measure with `TableFeedPerfMacTests`' harness (`EXACT_TABLE_PERF`, frames back to back, steadier under load than the window server) and with Animation Hitches interleaved with the hand-built table.
- **document-render's bar test fails on main** (2026-11-12, at `9e7a36d6`'s fixture): "JavaScript-rendered pages mark the bar, whose symbols are the bar's size before the page adopts and after" fails at "menu before adoption". Its fixture moved the navigation root to the view's root for `8db3f857`.
- **The router sweep has three failures of its own** (2026-11-12, seen landing the q0535 sweep fix; each also on its base). `host/web/tests/navigation.mjs`'s "Markdown reader only navigates supported parsed URL protocols" fails on both targets every run: it gets `HTTP://example.test/x` where it expects `http://…`, and relative links are not resolved to absolute URLs. On the JS target (`navigation::browser_session_history_on_the_js_target`) two cases fail now and then: "tabs retain ids and push history" (`select retained top pushes`, 3 !== 4; 1 of 9 runs on base), which reads `history.length` right after a tap without waiting for it to change, and "focused route teardown ignores retired blur but preserves live blur" (teardown console errors; 2 of 9).
- **The docs lookup test truncates the sheet recipe before its sheet declaration** (2026-11-12, reproduced with origin/main files). `bun test scripts/exact.test.mjs --test-name-pattern "docs prints"` fails because `exact docs sheet` prints the recipe's first 40 lines, before `navigationPresentation="sheet"`. Make the selected excerpt contain the requested example.
- **`check` reports a Markdown ordered or task list as clipped at the largest text size, where nothing is cut off** (2026-11-12, run). A `text markup="markdown"` holding `1. …` is "11 px wider than its box" at the web's 53 px root, and one holding `- [ ] …` 15 px; on an iOS 27.1 simulator at the largest accessibility size the task item is 4 pt wider. Screenshots on both hosts show every marker and line whole, and a bullet list is not reported. `verify` counts these as problems, and an agent spent three attempts on them. Each marker hangs in an indent of 40 that does not grow with the text (`LIST_INDENT`, `markdown/src/pieces.rs`), so test first whether `check` counts the marker's advance, its trailing space included. Make `check` count only ink outside the box.
- **An app's first agent drive on iOS stops as stale after building it** (2026-11-12, 8 of 9 drives). In apps made by `exact new` that had a web build and no iOS build, `bun exact.mjs agent ios …` built the app ("there is no build yet") and then stopped with exit 3, "ios build is stale", naming `target/bake-compiled/*.rs`. Run again, each drive worked. Make a drive's own build count as current.

- **Layout asks too many size questions** (2026-11-12, LLP 1165 D5). In a chain of flex containers the vendored engine measures a leaf 18 to 25 times where hooke measures it 3 or 4 times. Take upstream Taffy's #1221, #1332 and #1343 with the vendor refresh, then trace the remaining questions.
- **Aspect-ratio chains remake sizes at every level** (2026-11-12, LLP 1165 D5). With siblings, they add about six questions for each level above a node. Trace and reduce those questions.
- **A size-cache hit can lose a leaf's height proof** (2026-11-12, LLP 1165 §2.7, measured). `height_measured` is false where a layout with no size cache has it true in 18% of the random trees' layouts, before LLP 1165's change and after it (`size_cache_height_proof_losses`). Telling the kernel of every leaf hit through its measure closure made that 0.4% and cost up to half again on an edit. Try a notification carrying only the axis and whether the height was known. A hit at an ancestor asks the leaf nothing, so it cannot recover those.
- **Cephal's Chrome-recorded cases need a run against vendored Taffy** (2026-11-12, LLP 1165 D5). Run the 120 variants in its `TAFFY_DIVERGENCES.md`, which use Taffy's XML schema, and investigate disagreements.
- **Main-thread work remains after layout moved to the owner thread** (2026-11-12, LLP 1165 D5). The Swift apply, painter and Core Animation commit remain there. Build LLP 1072 stages 4 and 5 and prepare the painter's lines off main.
- **Multi-column relayout drifts by one or two ULPs** (2026-11-12, LLP 1165 D4). At baseline `d14d39d2`, seed 138, round 15, differs from rehydration by two ULPs in node 19's x position; seed 291, round 25, differs from replay by one ULP in node 32's x position. Both fail under the old cache too. `size_cache_policies_agree_on_all_generators` expects exactly these two seeds, with and without baseline alignment, until the drift is fixed. Repair the drift and remove the expectations together.
- **Layouts with no size cache disagree with fresh and cached baseline layouts** (2026-11-12, LLP 1165 D4, §5 Q4). `Off` differed from its own fresh layout in 138 of 1,536 baseline generator runs; `All` and `Nine` agreed with fresh layouts in that run. Cold baseline seeds 150 and 507 also differ between cached and uncached trees, with `All` and `Nine` agreeing. Trace both discrepancies and make the uncached reference consistent.
- **Android photos with `shutterSound: false` call an API absent below Android 13** (2026-11-12, reproduced on the API 31 JVM host). `Camera.kt` calls `MediaActionSound.mustPlayShutterSound()` without an SDK guard when the option is false; it throws `NoSuchMethodError` before `takePicture`. Guard that query on older supported Android versions.
- **Multi-column rules do not scroll with their columns** (2026-11-12, read, not run). Hosts draw `column-rule` from the column records in the scroll node's own layer, under its scroll view (`layoutColumnRules`, `host/apple/Sources/ExactKit/Columns.swift`). Drawing them in the scrolled content (on macOS the document view, offset by the node's own `kernelDocumentOrigin` as its boxes are; on iOS the `UIScrollView`) lets them scroll with their columns.
- **Browser test files that launch Chrome in `beforeAll` fail every test under Bun 1.4.2** (2026-11-12, run). `cdpBrowser` (`scripts/agent-launch.mjs`) registers its `afterAll(shared.close)` when it first launches; called inside `beforeAll`, the browser closes before the first test, which fails with "the DevTools pipe closed". `host/web/collection.test.mjs` now launches at the file's level, as `host/web-js/list.test.mjs` does; ten others still launch in `beforeAll` (`grep -rl "^  [a-z]* = await cdpBrowser(" host`), among them `collection-wait`, `documents-copy`, `documents-kept`, `request-refusal` and `shared-reorder`. Launch them at the file's level, or have `cdpBrowser` register its hook where Bun keeps it.
- **A throwing Android viewfinder hatch leaks its subscription** (2026-11-11, #241 review). On origin/main, `Camera.kt` calls `useCasesHatch` inside the consumer's `liveVideo`, after `VideoTracks.subscribe` registered the consumer and took its lease. If the hatch throws, `reconcile` drops the subscription without receiving its leave function. Reproduced on the JVM with origin/main's subscription and capture code and a throwing callback. Make that subscription transactional in a separate fix; rejected CameraX binds themselves remain non-throwing.
- **Apple test prerequisites and metadata** (2026-11-12, #366 validation). With freshly built Debug products, `host/apple/tests/menubar.test.mjs` stops at a missing `CExactLaunch` module before linking. Supply the current module and object dependencies. The iOS and watchOS cases of `host/apple/intents-metadata.test.mjs` disagree with Xcode 27.2 on an empty `systemProtocolMetadata` array; qualify the generator against that extractor.
- **Qualify Apple region-reader glyph positions around decorated atoms.** A Mac raster of `Before [Ab](badge:done) after`, repeated beyond 64 KB with underline and strike, differs slightly in horizontal glyph placement from the ordinary painter’s first line at the same 400 pt width and 16 pt font. Icon orientation and decoration exclusion agree after #130; the remaining whole-line pixel difference needs an isolated cause.
- **A grouped-list navigation test fails before its route opens** (2026-11-12, iOS 27, unchanged main at 35940005). `GroupedListIOSTests.testATappedRowStaysSelectedThroughThePushAndLetsGoOnReturn` fails before the pushed route appears; the other 47 tests pass. It also fails with #327's authored-box fix. Diagnose the route setup before changing grouped-row selection.

- **Apple's keyframes lowering (SVG and box motion) ends on the first of repeated 100% blocks** (2026-11-12, read, not run). `forward` in `host/apple/src/svg_lower.rs` builds its own track, so it neither merges a block naming the animation's timing function with one naming none nor holds the last block at offset 1, both of which `Keyframes::track` and `Animation::value_at` in `exact-motion` now do as Chrome does. Build the lowered track from `Keyframes::track`.
- **An iOS navigation-collapse test fails on main** (2026-11-12, run at 00037e6f on an iOS 27 simulator, twice, at low load). `NavigationCollapseIOSTests.testWrappedScrollerLosesAndRegainsTheHeaderWhenContentMovesAboveIt` fails after about 8 s: its route does not push, so it never reaches the scrolling it tests. The class's other 20 tests pass (`bun host/apple/build.mjs --test --ios --only NavigationCollapseIOSTests`).
- **An arranged pane whose height stops short of its grown allocation crashes its app** (2026-11-12, run on an iOS 27.1 iPhone Duo at b5ad88a1e3). With `max-height="280px"` on Duo Lab's Panes panes, `bun scripts/smoke.mjs duo --only arrangement` ends with "the app ended" in two runs of two, and passes without it: `EXC_BAD_ACCESS` in the stack guard, `Presenter.apply` nested through each batch's `defer`. `ArrangementHost.scrollers(in:top:)` gives a pane the route's bottom edge while it fills its told block. Padded for the tab bar, the pane is told 266 points, fills them and takes the edge; unpadded, it is told 291 and stops at 280, so it gives the edge back and the route pads again, and the told block alternates with the decision. LLP 1123 judges reaching an edge "laid out with zero clearance" (D3), which the padded layout cannot answer. Two ways out need a decision: judge a pane's reach from its unpadded block, or have the route leave the arrangement's edge to its panes as owners (D2), which the LLP does not say yet.
- **An arranged pane's handoff prefers a carried position to the one it saved** (2026-11-12, read, not run). When a pane rejoins `insetScrollers`, `ArrangementHost.syncInsets` starts its handoff from `node.pendingScrollTop ?? node.hiddenScroll?.y` (`ArrangementIOS.swift`), so a position carried from another scroll view while the saved one waits (`retainScrollPosition`) wins there, and `finishScrollHandoffs` may write it again over the restored one until UIKit settles the pane's safe area. While a saved position waits it is the reader's, so `retainScrollPosition` could carry nothing then. An authored write held for the pane lands in the batch where the pane rejoins, which drops the handoff for it; whether its position then moves with the inset change needs a run with an arrangement.
- **Apple's storage roots are one per process** (2026-11-12, read). `ROOTS` in `host/apple/src/picker.rs`, which answers `pickedPath`, `appRoots` and `appFile`, holds the roots of whichever app activated last, so a host process running two different apps' sessions can hand the earlier one the later one's directories. Key them by app.
- **Android headless carrier: a secret write fails with `sync /: Invalid argument`** (2026-11-12, #203). `bun exact.mjs test android --headless` on the OAuth fixture signs in and then cannot keep the session: the store's directory sync is refused (os error 22) on the emulator's filesystem. The APK carrier passes the same test. Evidence was an Android 16 (API 36.1) arm64 emulator.
- **Android headless carrier: the default store level refuses to start** (2026-11-12, #204). A TypeScript app built with `bun exact.mjs android --headless` and no `deploy.store.linux` stops at launch with "the baked store level requires the delivery adapter; regenerate the app entry"; with `"deploy": {"store": {"linux": "0"}}` (as apps/oauth-fixture has) it runs. Either the headless Android build should bake level 0 or the message should name the setting.
- **A native scroll-edge selection reaches Exact's layout only at the next authored batch, and its nil runs the passes inside the app's call** (2026-11-12, read, not run). `RouteController.setContentScrollView(_:for:)` moves UIKit's bar at once, but three things follow only at the next authored batch: the scroller the title collapses with, the scroll views UIKit insets, and the route's cover. So after a menu action selects an app-owned list, the authored list stays inset under the bar until something else changes. A nil runs the edge passes at once (`selectScrollEdges`), with three effects. Outside a batch the passes report covers whose answer is applied within the same call, so the route hatch can run inside the app's own `setContentScrollView`. The passes run during a push or pop, where `sync` defers its own. And the position `collapse` keeps for the scroller it leaves waits for the next batch, past any scroll the reader makes meanwhile. A selection the route hatch makes comes after `prepareRoutes` has passed that route, so a pushed route that selects an app-owned list arrives with its authored list still under the bar. A fix would make `collapse`'s choice in the call, so that UIKit gets Exact's scroller and the route its cover at once after a nil (`NativeListNavigationIOSTests` and `ContentOwnersIOSTests` assert both), and run the rest as an applied batch on the next turn, since a bare `sync` restores no positions: `syncNextTurn`'s, and the one `completeTransition` owes a push or pop, are both bare. A route whose hatch changed its selection needs its passes run again.
- **`ExactRoute.contentScrollView` is a snapshot of the route hatch's last call** (2026-11-12, read). It is written only in `moduleRoute`, so after a selection it is stale until Exact next prepares the route. Resolved on read, as `route.element(_:)` is, it would need a hatch-table entry and no timing rule.
- **Android: no sustained frame measurement** (2026-11-12, #231). `agent android perf frames live` is refused as unsupported and `dumpsys gfxinfo` saw three frames in five seconds of an infinite animation under the test carrier's virtual clock, so the 3D-card fix (7de13f61) has no before/after number on a device.
- **AppKit column rules sit below their column content** (2026-11-12, #245 verification). In a 390×80 scroller with two balanced columns of three 20-point rows, the window capture shows the 60-point rule starting 20 points below the rows, in both LTR and RTL. The rule is horizontally centered in the gap. Check the native rule layer’s vertical coordinate conversion; this drive used overlay scrollbars and did not diagnose the cause.
- **Warn about top-level Intl on Android without Intl** (#205). The bake knows the target, but inspects bytecode with the host Hermes. Pass the selected Android bundle’s receipt availability through the captured bake inputs (including receipt-change invalidation), then warn on global Intl uses during module initialization in app and imported code. Reuse the scope-aware TypeScript walker; cover guarded and deferred uses and both Android carriers. The pinned v6 bundle supplies Intl; this concerns local overrides that omit it.

- **OAuth credentials in Contract answers** (2026-11-12, #208). Neither `provider.accessToken(store)` nor `provider.idToken(store)` is guarded against being returned to Contract: both are ordinary strings with a documentation-only restriction. Decide whether to enforce that boundary.
- **The native prelude looks up a module's names in plain objects** (2026-11-12, read, not run). `js/src/prelude.js` `tool` reads `global.exact.tools[name]` and `failureCode` reads `FETCH_FAILURE[e.kind]`, so a handler name or a fetch failure kind of `constructor` or `toString` finds Object.prototype's member: `tool("constructor", args)` answers `Object(args)` where the web JS target now answers no handler (`host/web-js/ts-data.js`). Read each with `Object.hasOwn`, as host/web-js does.
- **Linux's `showPicker` and `saveFile` can ask the desktop portal too** (2026-11-12). The document pickers now ask `org.freedesktop.portal.FileChooser` (`host/painted/src/portal.rs`), while `input type=file`'s `showPicker` (LLP 1069.002 D8, which rules the portal) and `saveFile` (LLP 1069.010 D3) still refuse outside the agent. Their agent answers (`answer_picker`, `answer_save` in `presenter/picker.rs`) already copy a file in or out given a path, which is what `portal::choose` returns.
- **EAS worker qualification (#48).** Run the app-owned native build recipes on a real EAS worker with distribution signing, upload and store submission. Local artifact checks do not establish these.

- **Notification actions: what LLP 1133.004 stage 2 left unbuilt or unrun** (2026-11-12, expo/exact#127). Not built: actions on Android (no local notifications there yet; the same plan lists become `Notification.Action`s, a text action a `RemoteInput`, a foreground action a `PendingIntent` to `ExactActivity`, a background one a host-owned `BroadcastReceiver` that hands expedited work the headless session LLP 1133.017 stage 3 still owes; `authenticationRequired` is `setAuthenticationRequired`, `destructive` has no counterpart), Windows and the web; the dismissal (`customDismissAction`, the web's `close`); `requireInteraction`; a category a push-only app declares without writing a post; a macOS notification-specific cold launch that opens no window (ordinary startup still shows its window); D6's first-unlock rule for ordinary wake carriers (notification responses check the Keychain marker before boot). Not run: a background action on a phone, through APNs, from the Lock Screen with `authenticationRequired`, and with a request that outlives the system's time; macOS's actions through Notification Center (the delegate and runner are covered by XCTest).
- **`difftest explore … --js-only` drives every program and 18 diverge** (2026-11-12). Run over `contract/corpus apps/*/app.contract`, these remain undiagnosed. A module call is refused for a missing grant, which the runner answers as `prompt` (`contacts-calendar-fixture`, `geofence-fixture`, `photo-editor`, `photos-fixture`, `recorder`). The oracle has no answer for a source the runner never called (`bench` `portList`, `lights` `lights`). The driver's DOM has no `getBoundingClientRect` (`native-fixture`). The rest have no diagnosis yet: `agents`, `auth-fixture`, `expose`, `metronome`, `orientation-fixture`, `shelf`, `unsaid`, `grouped-touch`, `inline-image` and `share`. Each is either the driver's gap or the JS target's defect; tell which before fixing.
- **The workers publish an `exactBodyTo` file when an abort or a deadline lands between the body's end and the rename** (2026-11-12, read, not run). `host/executor/src/body_to.rs` checks for an abort around each read and not between the last one and `staged.publish()`. The asynchronous machine admits the publish under the executor's lock (`async_body_to.rs`); the workers could do the same.
- **The workers' retirement leaves a retired completion's accounting standing** (2026-11-12, read, not run). `complete` in `host/executor/src/lib.rs` returns at `state.retired` before it releases the run's bytes and counts, so they stay in the shared state for as long as a waker or a clone holds it. The asynchronous machine now applies physical ends after retirement (`async_retire.rs`).
- **`exactBodyTo` is refused on Apple's own asynchronous lane** (2026-11-12). The machine's portable lanes write a reply to a file (LLP 1162 D8 item 5); `DarwinLane`'s callbacks run on URLSession's delegate queue, which must not block on a disk, so it refuses by name. It needs a download task or writes handed to another thread before the machine can be measured on a device.
- **A resource that wakes is asked as a refresh** (2026-11-12, read, not run). A `when` that opens again asks its resource forced (`runner/src/runner/settlement.rs`, "Waking asks again, forced"; `r.asleep` in `host/web-js/rt.js`), and a forced ask bypasses the executor's cache (LLP 1162 D4): the HTTP cache natively, `cache: "reload"` on the web. LLP 1161.002 D3 asks only that no request is kept across idleness. Decide whether a wake is a refresh; a launch's own ask no longer is (LLP 1162.000 D1).
- **An upload test's fixture lacks `socketProtocols`** (2026-11-12, main 6674ae8c). `bun test host/web-js/upload.test.mjs` fails "an untraced async tool handler keeps its origin for fetches and nested module calls" with `SyntaxError: export 'socketProtocols' not found in './grant-admission.js'`: the fixture's copy of the runtime predates the WebSocket subprotocols change. Find what the fixture copies and give it the current file.
- **Shelf's test file fails three tests on macOS on main** (2026-11-12).
- **`exact-data` tests share machine state across checkouts** (2026-11-12). Its lock and kept-document tests fail when two checkouts on one machine run them at once.
- **`exact-painted --lib` exhausts worker slots at the default 96 test threads** (2026-11-12). The asynchronous-core tests run out of slots; use 16 threads until the tests isolate their workers.
- **Two failures in `exact-js` outside the gate** (2026-11-12, run on a clean origin/main `03f072db`). `cargo test -p exact-js --test it caltrain::identity_and_grants_are_the_bakes_and_the_modules_agree` fails: `781410aa` gave Caltrain `exactTime` and the test's list was not updated. `cargo clippy -p exact-js --all-targets -- -D warnings` fails at `js/src/bootstrap_tests.rs:111` (`needless_borrows_for_generic_args`). `exact-js` is not in `default-members`, so the six checks see neither; the async lane's `--workspace` runs do.
- **A launch's setting, where it is not yet given or named** (2026-11-12, LLP 1153.002). An iOS drive does not pass a marked name on as `SIMCTL_CHILD_<NAME>`, so setting it in the driver's environment still reads as a changed build. Only a `fetch` refusal names the setting an origin came from; a `net.websocket` one does not. The Rust render binaries read their own process's environment under `--serve`, which `serve`'s check (LLP 1153 D4.6) does not cover.

- **The photo-upload executor test can miss the uploads it must feed** (2026-11-11). `a_burst_of_photo_uploads_goes_three_at_a_time_and_leaves_other_streams_their_places` gathers open uploads before feeding them; uploads that open after that gather get no feed, and the test waits five seconds for an outcome that needs one. It failed 7 of 12 runs of the unchanged executor on a 96-core Linux machine.
- **The executor's quit test fails under a parallel run** (2026-11-11). `a_quit_ends_every_group` fails intermittently on the 96-core Linux machine.
- **The incremental runner test fails for geofence-fixture on Linux** (2026-11-11). `runner/tests/it/incremental.rs` fails for `geofence-fixture` at `18b358e0`.
- **App icons not yet seen running** (2026-11-11, LLP 1163 §4). tvOS's brand assets and the watch app's icon compile, and `assetutil` shows them, but no Apple TV or watch simulator runtime was installed to see them on a Home Screen. The Windows icon resource is linked only by a build on Windows, which has not run. The Android drive's check for an APK older than its sources (`exact-build.json`) counts no icon file, so an edit to an icon alone leaves the old APK looking current.
- **The Android build does not compile on origin/main** (2026-11-11, found building LLP 1163's icons). `host/painted/src/lower.rs:326`, Android-only, passes `n.style.cx` by value to `point`, and `Dimension` is no longer `Copy` since `2c765f970`. Taking `&Dimension`, returning `*v`, and calling `point(&n.style.cx)` and `point(&n.style.cy)` compiles (tried, not kept).
- **The branded-Chrome cleanup test counts other drives' bundle copies** (2026-11-11). `scripts/agent-browser.test.mjs`'s "branded Chrome removes its bundle copies after script and test shutdown" (macOS, `CHROME` naming branded Chrome) compares the shared `code_sign_clone` directory before and after; a Chrome another drive starts meanwhile and keeps open fails it. Check only the copies its own browsers made.
- **An inline Tab stop on the page joins a dialog popover's focus search** (2026-11-11, seen while fixing Tab from a negative-index control). `host/painted/src/presenter/typing.rs` `focus_stop` admits any inline Tab stop wherever its paragraph is, so when a `dialog` popover opens (`alert.rs:268`, which passes only the dialog's boxes), an inline link on the page can be chosen as the dialog's first stop. Restrict inline stops to paragraphs among the candidates, as starting points already are. Separately, `alert.rs:268` still takes a fragment's starting point when a dialog opens, though HTML's dialog focusing steps do not use one.
- **An app's `console` in an Android release build's logcat** (2026-11-12, LLP 1027.009 D3 and Q4). Apple, Linux and Windows write an app's `console` lines to the system's log in every build. Android writes them in a development build only (`EXACT_LOG_CONSOLE`, which the reader sets; `host/painted/src/android.rs`, `console`), under the tag `exact-app`. The design asks for every build and a tag that is the app's name. The Android path is compiled for `aarch64-linux-android` and no device has run it.
- **Android's journal echo skips a replaced runner's first lines** (2026-11-13). `EXACT_LOG_JOURNAL=1` (`host/painted/src/canvas/journal.rs`) keeps one count of the lines it has written for the process, and a development reload or an applied update starts a journal at zero: the new journal's lines up to the old count are not echoed. Keep the count with the runner it counts.
- **Android select menu (#243).** Replace the page-painted menu with LLP 1115.001 D10's anchored popup window, including touch scrolling, accessible option rows, focus restoration and the platform's transitions. A menu left open while layout moves its control paints from the previous frame's geometry, but taps use the new geometry. Long menus cannot be scrolled by touch (also documented in the Android README).
- **Later SVG switch children lose reference targets** (2026-11-11). `host/svg-file/src/lower.rs` skips every child after a switch takes one. `<switch><rect width="5" height="10"/><rect id="r" width="10" height="10"/></switch><use href="#r" x="5"/>` draws only the first rectangle. Reproduced with the lowerer at `9e53578f22` and after the `systemLanguage` fix; retain unselected children for references without drawing them in the switch.
- **Tamber parameter range probes omit package tokens** (2026-11-11). With `brand = reading.card` supplied by a package and `Card.inset: length = 8px in 0..24`, the server accepts `set-param Card.inset=99px` while the build refuses it. `contract/cli/src/tamber_ops.rs` passes the app design alone to `written_ranges`; its probes cannot fold the package reference and return no range errors. Join the package tokens in those probes as in the ordinary fold.
- **Swatch's Apple fixture needs native targets.** `packages/fixtures/swatch/test/apple.mjs` calls the Apple builder directly after `exact new`, whose default app has no Apple crate. Enroll the platforms the suite runs before building; today it stops at `Cargo has no swatchfixture-apple target`.

- **Virtualized iOS snap geometry (#219):** unmounted rows do not contribute a target on a descendant of the row root, a root displaced by a transform, a root with a font-relative or percentage inset or margin, or a target farther than the bounded window from the projection. The synchronous drag-end query examines at most 64 rows; with sparse targets, the nearest one can lie outside that window.

- **With an app's own delegate on a stack, a pop UIKit makes there holds `changing` until Exact next hears a call** (2026-11-12, read, not run). Exact hears a stack's pops only through the delegate calls it forwards. Under an app's own delegate it hears none of that stack's, so the popped routes are in flight (`routesInFlight`), `changing` is true and the agent reads `in-progress`, and `popMayBegin` refuses the stack's back swipe, until the next tab delegate call or the turn after the next sync (`followPopsNextTurn`) reports the pop. Under the agent's frozen timing, an input in that state that succeeds and brings no batch waits its two-second bound and replies `settled: false, reason: "transition"`, as `clock settle` does. Following a stack whose delegate the app replaced needs a call of UIKit's that Exact still hears, such as the route's own appearance callbacks.
- **UIKit logs "Unbalanced calls to begin/end appearance transitions" for a `RouteController` in a drive through More** (2026-11-14, Duo Lab on the iPhone 18 Pro simulator, iOS 27.0, `--chrome platform`, on `main` `6feefeb3c9`). UIKit logged the line in some runs of a drive that picks Keyboard from More's list, settles, and takes the agent's `back` to the list. It is not known which appearance call is unbalanced, or whether the row's pick or More's pop to its list makes it; `xcrun simctl spawn <udid> log show --predicate 'eventMessage CONTAINS "Unbalanced calls"'` over the drive finds it.
- **Duo Lab's Panes title draws over its first line on the iPhone 18 Pro simulator** (2026-11-11, iOS 27.0, `main` `35ac89eb7b`, `--chrome platform`). On Panes' root, and on `panes-next` ("Pushed panes"), the large title overlaps the text "Responsive layout. Split and Overlay require the native iOS app on iOS 27.1 or later." at the top of the screen.
- **Android status bar scope when native sheets land** (LLP 1105 D7, #209). The presenter resolves `modal` routes as covering pages today. When Android reports native presentations, use their coverage, detent/width and settled transition state; iOS leaves the bar behind a medium or iPad sheet and holds the committed style through transitions. Android still ignores `status-bar-animation`. Qualify the independent bar flags on a phone in both schemes, across routes and a sheet; no device/emulator run was available for this change.
- **iOS launch after `didFinishLaunching` (LLP 1047.001.001 Q2).** Find what moved about 16 ms after the callback when SafariServices was linked, in the phone measurements of §2.2.
- **SafariServices for other Apple entries (LLP 1047.001.001 Q3).** Measure an app extension and the watch companion before extending the device app executable’s launch link to them. An App Clip copies its parent’s executable and carries the link unmeasured.
- **Permission resources on return from Settings** (2026-11-11, found in #217). Android does not re-ask `enumerateDevices` after the camera is allowed in Settings. Announcing it on resume must wait for the camera provider: before it is ready the list has no cameras, so a bare invalidation would drop the anonymous entry. The JVM tests have no fixture for a provider with a camera. Android's `Pedometer.kt` does not invalidate `pedometerStatus` on resume, and `SpeechRecognition.kt` does not refresh `speechRecognitionStatus`. Apple's media-capture module does not invalidate `enumerateDevices` on return to the foreground either.
- **Tests seen failing while landing issue fixes that the fixes did not touch** (2026-11-11). `exact-painted`'s `agent::tests::exit::an_orderly_exit_waits_for_a_request_with_effects` (`host/painted/src/agent/tests/exit.rs:216`, `must_finish` still true after the exit's bound) failed 1 run in 20 on origin/main at a load average near 15, and 5 in 12 near 40. `pump::held_work_tests::a_wake_without_a_reply_releases_held_work` and `pump::abort_tests::an_abort_whose_answer_was_replaced_is_still_cancelled_on_an_empty_pump` failed in whole-suite runs and passed alone. `scripts/agent-launch.test.mjs` "a native build is stale when a setting its module inlined has another value now" failed once in a run of the whole file and passed in the next three. The first run of `host/web-js/build.test.mjs` in a checkout whose web compiler is not yet built fails about fourteen tests in a millisecond each, and the second run passes. `PressFocusIOSTests.testADismissedSheetHandsNothingOn` fails on origin/main's own sources on an iOS 27.0 simulator, alone as well as in its class. `contract`'s `growth` test `resource_clock_advice_grows_with_derives` (`contract/cli/tests/growth.rs:130`, a ratio of two best-of-three timings near 150 µs and 1 ms held to 6.0) failed at 6.9 in a whole-suite run and in one of three runs alone. `host/web-js/answer-timers.test.mjs` "a live page waits the full delay after an async answer resumes" failed 14 runs in 24 alone, at a load average under 10: the two timers armed after the answer resumes are due in 500 and -500 ms where the test expects 2000 and 1000, as if measured from when the page's clock last moved and not from now. If that is the runtime and not the test's paused clock, a live page's timer set after a long wait fires early. `scripts/agent-test.test.mjs` "authored media preferences apply before observations, override by name, and survive reload" expects `prefer: unknown dark: expected`, and the message now carries a `did you mean` hint before `expected`. `exact-executor`'s `stream::tests::a_burst_of_photo_uploads_goes_three_at_a_time_and_leaves_other_streams_their_places` failed once in a whole-suite run of `exact-apple` and once of `exact-painted` (each then compiled it), at load averages near 25 to 40, and passed five times alone. `contract`'s `growth` test `resource_clock_advice_grows_with_functions` failed once in a whole-suite run and passed three times alone.
- **Setup's cargo advice on Windows can blame the wrong file** (2026-11-11, read in #3, not run). `cargoProxyFault` (`scripts/exact.mjs`) probes whatever `cargo` the shell runs and then looks up `cargo.exe` across the whole `PATH`. Windows runs the first match by `PATHEXT` in each directory, so with a `cargo.cmd` shim ahead of rustup's `cargo.exe` the fault names the working proxy as the one that refuses `+<pin>` and skips its directory. Resolving the failing file as the shell does, and comparing directories by real path without case, would close it.
- **A standalone app whose first boot is refused draws nothing** (2026-11-11, found in #163). A Rust source whose `answer` returns `Unavailable` at boot refuses the boot. A release iOS build then shows a blank view (the session is `failed` with the reason in `bootError`), Linux prints `exact: boot: …` and exits, and the web JS target empties its root. LLP 1031 D8 leaves the previous generation or the embedder's fallback visible; a standalone app's first boot has neither. What it should show is undecided.
- **An inline run's opacity: what is left** (2026-11-11). Apple and the painted host group a faded run's colour, background, shadow, stroke and decorations. Left: a content region's retained picture does not follow an opacity animation (its run's or its paragraph's own), as it is not recaptured while the paragraph is unchanged; inside an opacity group, a decorated stroked or emoji run is drawn by the clipped whole line, so a neighbour's ink that crosses into its advance is drawn with it (Core Text paints those glyphs in invisible mode); an infinitely animated run re-sends its paragraph every frame, as a changing run colour already does.
- **Web JS sends before source activation refuse finite arguments** (2026-11-11). When `data.answer()` returns `null`, `host/web-js/rt.js` refuses the send's action instead of waiting for `data.ready()`. Reproduced on `db0f0618` and with the non-finite argument fix; the runner already waits for activation.
- **Android's island pictures, what an emulator showed and what a phone still owes** (2026-11-11, LLP 1159.000 D1; `issues/closed/20261110-android-recorder-drops-island-images.md`). On an API 36 emulator a page with `text-shadow`, `-webkit-text-stroke`, `background-clip: text`, a `clip-path`, a `mask-image` and a blurred SVG shape draws as the web's does, and a blended SVG element draws unblended and says so once in the log, as the schema's `mix_blend_mode` row records. Not measured: what a shadow or a stroke that animates costs, since each paint sends a new picture; and nothing was seen on a phone's GPU.
- **An outside app's Rust workspace after its checkout moves, and a bake source built with arguments** (2026-11-11, #163). After exact2 moves, `bun exact.mjs update` repairs `exact-*` paths and leaves any other exact2 crate (`contract`) on the old path, so Cargo cannot read it; the way out is to write that path as `<exact2>/…` again and run `update`. `buildModule` (`host/web-js/module.mjs`) copies only a path, `::default()` or `::new()` into the module it generates, so a data source constructed with arguments needs a crate of the app's own named in `rust.module.package`. Its matcher reads the raw script, so a commented-out call it can copy is taken ahead of a live one it cannot, as on origin/main; that is left for the real parse. A fix for either needs edits made on a real parse of the manifest, and of the build script, not on patterns over their text.

- **Native executor test isolation on a shared Mac** (2026-11-11). Default-concurrency runs can exhaust test worker slots and hit request or exit deadlines in `exact-painted` and `exact-apple`. The branch's full root and Linux test commands pass with four test threads; the baseline Apple library also hits a process deadline. Qualify test worker admission and real-time waits under suite load.
- **Android extension writers that still follow a link planted after the path is answered** (2026-11-11, #164). Each needs app code holding `fs.write` on the directory to act in the moment between the context's check and the write. The writes Exact's own Kotlin makes go through `Files.output`/`Files.write` (`O_NOFOLLOW`), but three do not: the camera on a device, where CameraX opens the answered `File` itself when the shutter finishes (hand it a stream or descriptor opened with no-follow before `takePicture`; CameraX is not in every machine's Gradle cache, so it was not compiled with the rest); the recording encoder's output on a device, which `MediaMuxer` and `RandomAccessFile` open by path (`Recording.kt` `Encoder`, `Parts.kt` `PartWriter` and `mux`'s `.mp4.tmp`); and a parent directory swapped for a link, which is followed both inside `picker::place` itself (`host/painted/src/picker.rs` `place_from` checks the parts one at a time by path, and `file` joins the path again after `open_from`, so a directory replaced by a link between two lookups answers a path that already goes through it) and at any later write, a speech transcript's directory during a running recognition included, since `O_NOFOLLOW` guards only the last name. The fix for all of them is one thing: a descriptor-relative walk from the app root (`openat` with `O_NOFOLLOW|O_DIRECTORY`, as ibex2's `AppDirectories` does) with the parent's descriptor held through the write.
- **iOS: qualify a resize's batches on a Duo simulator** (2026-11-10). A native container now takes its new size before any batch lays it out, and a route whose controller pins its appearance is reported after a flip (XCTests on an iPhone simulator). Left: drive refresh through folding, rotation, resizing and appearance flips on a Duo simulator.
- **iOS: a refresh control there at boot is hosted by the navigation bar on an iPhone Duo** (2026-11-10, seen in a probe app on the 27.1 simulator). The stack is laid out before its window, and UIKit moves the control into the folded display's fixed 58-point large-title bar. Not yet compared with a hand-built app on the same OS; if its control stays in the scroll view, this is a tell.
- **`packages/background-transfer/test/apple.mjs macos` fails four assertions** (2026-11-11; two runs at be826004 on macOS 27.0.1): the upload's response body, download `d1`, folder `f1` and appear `f2`. The upload's `uploaded` was 8 of 8 in both; an earlier run read 0 (the carrier's `sent`, `host/apple/Sources/ExactKit/Transfers.swift`), so that count may be intermittent. The suite had not run since `exact new` stopped naming Apple by default. A first `host/apple/build.mjs` of a fresh `exact new` app is refused by the driver as stale: the build writes `.exact/shells` after the `started` its receipt records (`build.mjs` takes it from the process's start); the suite now writes the shells first.
- **A placed canvas child's layout change, with VoiceOver running** (2026-11-11, #68). On iOS a canvas whose surface places its children posts one `.layoutChanged` when the set or order of its exposed children changes, and nothing while they only move. It posts behind a dialog's sheet and under `inert` too, so a culled card there makes VoiceOver read the screen again once: the accepted cost of a rule with no reachability state. Listen for that re-read with VoiceOver on a phone. The Mac still posts `.layoutChanged` for every placed child whose placement changed, each frame (`GpuMac.swift` `readPlacements`, `NodeView.placementChanged`); a fix wants VoiceOver on a Mac to judge which element to name.
- **A look's `color` under a boundary on the wasm web host** (2026-11-12, #111). `Host::emit_looks` writes only `rules`, never `colour_below`, so a mode or `color-scheme` boundary below a hovered or focused node keeps its own colour where the JS target gives it the look's (from the code). Not built here: on this Mac the pinned nightly's `rust-lld` aborts linking a wasm app (`Library not loaded: @rpath/libLLVM.dylib`; the toolchain has it in `lib/`, not in `lib/rustlib/aarch64-apple-darwin/lib/`).
- **Where a JavaScript render still differs from the render host** (2026-11-11, found in #108; `host/web-js/render-settle.test.mjs` `DIFFERS` pins each). The render host rejects a refused storage call; a JavaScript render leaves it unanswered. So a source that catches the refusal answers its fallback there and stays pending here (as LLP 1048.000 D9 has a refused source keep its placeholder), and a stream whose mapper awaits a refused call, one it makes itself or one made before the stream opened, ends its message there (a settled 200) and holds the stream to the deadline here (a 503). Rejecting the refused call here, as the render host does, would close both; it is not done, since a rejection nothing handles in a render ends the `serve` process today.
- **A detached rejection on a server promise ends a JavaScript render's process** (2026-11-11, found reviewing the render store's refusal). `host/web-js/render.mjs` hands each render's realm the server's own `fetch`, `Request`, `Response`, streams, `crypto.subtle` and `WebAssembly`, so their promises, and every promise chained on them, belong to the server's realm. When one is left rejected with a value made outside the render's realm, as `new Response('body').text().then(() => { throw 'x'; })` or a refused `store.set` caught and rethrown as its message, `render.mjs`'s `unhandledRejection` listener cannot tell which render owns it and keeps Bun's fatal handling, and the process exits. The string rethrow exits it on main too. Containing it needs every promise a render sees made in its own realm (each promise-returning server API wrapped, and the objects they return) or renders run where an unhandled rejection ends only that render.
- **`exact-painted`'s timing tests fail under a loaded Mac** (2026-11-12). `cargo test -p exact-painted --lib` on an M5 running other builds fails one or two of `presenter::pump`'s `an_abort_whose_answer_was_replaced_is_still_cancelled_on_an_empty_pump` and `a_wake_without_a_reply_releases_held_work`, and what is now `exact-executor`'s `async_tests::the_suite_passes_on_the_asynchronous_core`, a different one each run (main failed one of five runs); each passes alone. Find the wall-clock wait each takes and give it the test clock (`docs/testing.md`). `agent::tests::exit::an_orderly_exit_waits_for_a_request_with_effects` is another (2026-11-12): it failed twice in full `--lib` runs and passes alone.
- **`exact-executor`'s tests fail together under load** (2026-10-27, a Mac Studio shared with long Lean lanes). A run of them failed nine socket and idle tests (`host/executor/src/socket_tests.rs`, "an outcome within five seconds"; `idle_tests.rs`) and `the_suite_passes_on_the_asynchronous_core`; each passes alone. A short read no longer zeroes its unused buffer tail on every read (2026-11-12), which cleared the large-body timeout under contention, but the nine were not reproduced and a contended run still fails the child-process `maxBuffer` test. Find what each five-second wait waits on before lengthening it.
- **Three press tests cannot open their page on this Mac** (2026-11-12, main f72a364c). In `host/web/tests/press.test.mjs`, "only the innermost pressable shows the press…", "a pressable that declares no feedback dims while pressed…" and "the pressed paint shows by the press rule…" fail in about 2 ms with `the DevTools pipe closed (Target.createTarget)` (`open`, line 147, through `agent-launch.mjs:484`), every run; the other 27 pass. The fleet's Chromium runs them. Find which browser `open` launches there and why its pipe closes.
- **The web JS target still handles navigation roots the compiler refuses** (2026-11-12, `8db3f857`). A root is now only the view's root, so no plan holds a root inside a route or two roots, yet `host/web-js/document.js` `place` lets ids show through an inactive nested root, and `host/web-js/src/answers.rs` looks for panel ids across other navigation owners. Remove what serves only those shapes.
- **Three web-js dev-loop tests fail on main on this Mac** (2026-11-12, 863ec1a1). In `host/web-js/dev-gallery.test.mjs`, both gallery cases ("the gallery: made only once a page asks through the gate…" and "a gallery that can't be made says so on its stream only…") time out after about 300 s in `dev-gallery.test.mjs:71`'s wait, warm or cold. In `host/web-js/dev-page-streams.test.mjs`, "a page whose app replaces fetch still registers and inspects" fails at line 152. Find the commit that changed each.
- **A zero-viewport glue test fails on main** (2026-11-12, 841ff596). `host/web/tests/zero-viewport-glue.test.mjs`'s "pre-rendered children do not authorize runner reports; a running runner receives only positive resizes" expects no reports at line 120 and receives three `["page"]` reports. Find the commit that changed it.
- **Rerunning authored tests can delete a surviving browser's profile** (2026-11-12, a review of 0e097be7). `sweepTestStores` (`scripts/agent-test.mjs`) removes a test store whose driver PID is dead without asking whether a Chrome still holds it; check the store's browser owner and `SingletonLock` as a named store's drive does before removing it.
- **A game proof's Linux and web legs fail on main** (2026-11-12, f20a7fff). `game/proof-snapshot-build.test.mjs`'s "a proof leaves ordinary builds usable and repins changed logic and added art" fails: the web crate does not compile (`exact_logic` not found) and its paranoid pass reports "no completed reconstruction samples".
- **The first macOS game proof in a new worktree trips its own input guard** (2026-11-12). The first build there creates `host/apple/.build/` and the root `target/`, which changes shared source directories' mtimes, so the proof refuses with "authored proof inputs changed during build"; the next run passes. The guard should not count directories a build creates outside the game's authored inputs.
- **Two photos seams checks assume a 2× Mac** (2026-11-12, 17f16354). `bun packages/photos/test/seams.mjs macos` expects the module to draw `swatch:blue at 100x40` and `swatch:red at 80x60` (`seams.mjs:284`, `:285`); this Mac draws them at 50×20 and 40×30. Take the scale from the session instead of assuming 2.
- **The APK lock test needs a JDK on a Mac without one** (2026-11-12). `scripts/agent-launch.test.mjs`'s "an APK with Maven libraries refuses missing locks before any native build work" fails where `java` is unusable: `buildApk` (`scripts/agent-android.mjs:787`) asks for a JDK before it reads the locks. Read the locks first, as the test's name says, or skip the test without a JDK.
- **LLP 1133.021 on Apple: drive 1 and drive 3's wheel need an unlocked Mac** (2026-10-27). With the console session locked, MapKit places no annotation view (its frame stays at the origin) and draws no marker, so `tap map/ferry` has nothing to hit; the window server gives no capture either. Run `bun packages/maps/test/map-demo.mjs macos` on an unlocked Mac: its marker selection and wheel zoom are unchecked. `tree` on Apple now lists a package view's declared parts, as the web does.
- **A Windows receipt can certify a source Cargo did not recompile** (2026-11-12). Windows build receipts record each baked source by content (`scripts/cargo-dep-info.mjs`), refreshing a cached digest when size or mtime is unchanged but ctime moved. An edit that keeps a Rust file's size and mtime is not seen by Cargo's fingerprint, so the crate is reused while the receipt records the edited source's hash and its checks pass on a stale executable. Recompile the units whose recorded contents changed (build-script helpers too) before writing the receipt, and test by rebuilding and running after such an edit.
- **iOS: a `min-height` percentage on a list row drops the platform's row height in the kernel** (2026-11-10). With iOS's metrics, `button "Row" min-height="50%"` in an `inset-grouped` list is laid out 19.2 points tall against 52 without it, while UIKit draws its own cell at about 52: the percentage replaces the platform's minimum and has nothing to resolve against. `inherited::a_percentage_min_height_on_a_list_row_has_no_basis` (`host/apple/tests/it/inherited.rs`) expects the two heights equal; it passes on a Mac, where `host/apple/src/scale.rs` `kernel` sets the Mac column, and fails on Linux, which builds with iOS's. A fix that keeps the sheet's minimum when the percentage has no basis (2026-11-11, three review rounds, not landed) grew to 19 files with Taffy patches, and still missed: a row with `delete=` (lowered into a swipe scroll, so `row_minimum` in `kernel/src/grouped.rs` finds the section, not the list, and `restyle` never reaches the swipe content); a comparison inside arithmetic (`calc(min(30px, 50%) + 0px)` gave 52 points where `min(30px, 50%)` gave 30, since a sum takes `value()`); `calc(0% + 0px)`, which a zero-percentage guard skipped; and a destructive `link` on the Mac, given the button metric (the sheet applies it to `button` alone). Start from the swipe scroll's parent and carry the fallback through arithmetic.
- **Should hit slop keep a button native?** (2026-11-12, from #103's hit-slop item). Hit slop never reaches a native button: `appearance="auto"` with `-exact-hit-slop` is refused (`lower-button-style-attr`; hit slop is not in `NATIVE_ROWS`, `contract/lower/src/controls.rs`), and on a `button` with no `appearance` it makes the compiler build a custom button, so adding slop changes how the button looks, which LLP 1115's "would someone who knows the platform notice?" forbids. A person decides whether a native button takes slop. If it does, the web needs the button's face in its own clipped box with the slop outside it: `#exact-root [data-native] { all: revert }` (`host/web/index.html`) outranks the hit-slop rule's `position: relative`, and the button's `overflow: clip` would clip the slop. A lane built that wrapper (2026-11-11, not landed); its reviewers found a checked menu row's `::after` mark escaping the clip, an auto-width flex button cut at its longest word, and a grouped row's separator lead lost through the wrapper.
- **Web: a raster menu row's checkmark in a centred flex row or under a bound direction** (2026-11-12). `host/web/neutral.css` places a raster-faced menu row's mark 1.5em before the start of its content box, so a row written `display="flex"` with `justify-content` `center` or `end`, or `flex-direction="row-reverse"`, puts it beside the face's first item rather than after the row's padding. The gutter takes the author's left or right padding by `--exact-separator-dir`, which a bound `direction` on the JS target does not write, so there the gutter can take the other side's padding (the mark still clears the face).
- **The persisted-time warning misses a sum of two readings** (2026-11-11, found in #102). `contract/lower/src/warnings.rs` `pair` gives a time plus a time the kind of a time, and a time less a time a span, so `performanceNow() + performanceNow() - start`, and `banked` added to it, are kept unwarned though they hold a launch-relative time. A sum of two times wants its own kind, which less one time is still a time. A rewrite that tracks each value's clock coefficient (2026-11-11, three review rounds, about 960 lines in `warnings/clock.rs`; not landed) still warned falsely on a duration plus `time.epochAtZero` (an absolute instant), a duration times or divided by an unseen field (`(performanceNow() - start) * time.bpm`), `time.epochAtZero + performanceNow() - saved.at`, equal decimal scales (`performanceNow() * 0.0001 - start * 0.0001`) and scales through a built-in (`/ floor(1000)`), and missed a self-referential state (`origin = origin > 0 ? origin : time.epochAtZero`) sent with `performanceNow()` added. A coefficient of zero must stay zero under any finite scale.
- **What `host.web.head` should admit of a stylesheet, an import map and `meta http-equiv`** (2026-11-11, PR #124, whose comments hold the measurements). `scripts/web-head.mjs` refuses only what Exact writes or reads itself, so each is written as given into the shell and every pre-rendered page. A same-origin `link rel="stylesheet"` applies, though an author's CSS is Contract's. An `importmap` mapping `./app.js` to another file makes the runtime load that file wherever no CSP is sent (`serve`, the dev loop, a static host); the render server's policy (`host/render/src/csp.rs`) ignores the inline map. A `meta http-equiv` `refresh` navigates, and a `Content-Security-Policy` one can stop `app.js`, with or without that policy. Each is the maintainers' decision.

- **Issue fixes written and not landed** (2026-11-10). Each stopped after three review rounds with a defect still open, or waits on a decision; each is a local branch of the checkout the issue work ran in, one commit unless said. #36's warning for a root child no host draws, `wave2/contract` fa8a7d9c. #34's deferred autofocus, `wave2/canvas` 44131ce3. #2's XCTest, `held/issue-2-xctest`.
- **Fixes for the 2026-11-10 review's issues, written and not landed** (2026-11-11). Each is a branch of the fleet checkout the fix lanes ran in, and its issues stay open. `fix/f-doc-watch-inotify-q` (`doc-watch-keeps-dead-inotify-watch-for-replaced-folder`, `recursive-doc-watch-walks-its-tree-on-every-commit`): Linux document watches recover after a folder is replaced and skip the walk on an unchanged commit. After `/docs/sub` is renamed to `away`, a new subscription to `/docs/away/a.md` shares the old descriptor and never fires (`host/painted/src/doc_watch.rs:325`); with `MAX_FOLDERS` full, deleted children of a recursive watch stay in `truncated` and grow without bound (`:293`); a folder moved out of a recursive root keeps its stale descriptor, so writes under its new path announce the old topic (`:329`, `prune` at `:361`).
- **Markdown decorations of one text share a shadow step** (2026-11-11, Astra's review of the line-through fix). `text "~~HHHH~~ ~~HHHH~~" markup="markdown" font-size=40 color="#ff0000" text-shadow="-60px 0px 0px #00ff00"` on the Linux host: the two strikes share one `source` (`host/painted/src/paint/text_decoration.rs:75`), so their shadows merge and paint at the first span's step, and the first red strike covers the second strike's green shadow where they overlap. Give each Markdown decoration span its own identity, so each shadow paints at its own first run.
- **Defects that reviewers of the review's fixes noticed and nobody has verified** (2026-11-11). Reproduce each by running before filing it (`docs/issues.md`); a later round of the fix that surfaced one may have fixed it. Line numbers are as the reviewer saw them.
  - Compiler and semantics. The resource clock advice resolves names by spelling: `fn performanceNow(): number = 7` passed as `performanceNow()` to a resource warns `analyze-resource-clock`, and `fn elapsed(): number = performanceNow()` beside `derive elapsed = 0` leaves `read(elapsed())` silent (`contract/analyze/src/lib.rs:862`, `:925`). An app syntax error under `contract verify` with a property file prints two paths, `PROPERTY REFUSED <test>:<app>:4:20` (`semantics/difftest/src/verify.rs:89`).
  - Runner. A refused `link_activities` leaves the snapshot and its `linked` flag true, so a later `set_activities` returns `Ok(None)` while the resource shows `linked` false (`runner/src/runner/activities.rs:65`). An answer with `exact:stale` 60000 followed by a plain answer before expiry keeps the old deadline (`runner/src/runner/modules/stale.rs:24`). Text under `visibility="collapse"` stays selectable, where `hidden` does not (`runner/src/instance/text.rs:158`). Two same-group voices at one start, the later retracted before it starts: the earlier stays cut and never sounds (`runner/src/sound.rs:553`, `host/web-js/sounds.js:103`).
  - Web. Appending to a virtualized list with `scrollFollowEnd=true scroll-behavior="smooth"` on the JS target corrects without `smooth`, so the follow jumps (`host/web-js/list.js:393`; both reviewers). A finite send while `data.answer` returns null is refused with "its source is not ready", where the runner parks it until `data_ready` (`host/web-js/rt.js:586`); a worker-placed source that answers `Infinity` fails with `NonFiniteValue` (`data/src/envelope.rs:127`). A markdown field's `beforeunload` does not run after `markup-editor.js` replaces the textarea (`host/web-js/rt.js:918`). `exact_set_time` is not called when `Date.now()` jumps and the zone offset does not, so the epoch at clock zero stays the launch epoch (`host/web/glue.js:680`). An accepted `.tiff`, `.tif` or `.markdown` file is delivered as `application/octet-stream` (`host/web/picker-glue.js:278`; both reviewers). An `img` with `visibility="collapse"` is still chosen as the document hero (`host/web/src/document_before.rs:77`). A `--render rust` page's text with `:hover` has no `data-exact-looks` before adoption (`host/web/src/document.rs:922`). A dist holding `assets/.config` or `assets/.well-known/data.json` makes `listPublicFiles` throw (`host/web/serve.mjs:400`).
  - Painting hosts. A `g` with `fill="light-dark(…)"` inside an `svg` with `color-scheme="dark"` paints the light colour (`host/painted/src/paint/svg.rs:491`). A shape outside the view box of an `svg` with `overflow="scroll"` never moves when the element scrolls (`host/painted/src/paint.rs:1154`). A field paints no text shadow (`host/painted/src/paint/field.rs:551`). Under `direction="rtl"` a native `button text-align="start"` with a text child aligns left (`host/painted/src/paint/button.rs:815`).
  - Apple and Android. The draw thread's `measure` and the main thread's `apply` mutate `CanvasText.fonts` with no lock (`host/apple/Sources/ExactSurfaces/Canvas2DText.swift:34`). A scroller's own `padding-top` or height change adjusts its anchored offset (`host/apple/Sources/ExactKit/ScrollAnchoring.swift:163`; both reviewers; fixed on the painting hosts). An `svg`'s shape past the view box shows in the element's padding (`SvgScene.swift:255`). A negative used `scroll-padding` shifts the port, where a browser uses 0 (`IntoView.swift:39`). `copyNativeResources` copies a `.DS_Store` in a declared resources tree into the bundle (`host/apple/assets.mjs:662`). The speech splitter appends an empty second piece for `'a'.repeat(16384) + ' '` (`packages/speech-recognition/apple/SpeechRecognition.swift:399`, `android/SpeechRecognition.kt:435`), and the result window counts characters, not bytes (`web/index.js:56`, `android/SpeechRecognition.kt:401`).
  - Packages. A web HID reader with `history:1024` after 1,025 reports returns 1,024 with `dropped:0` (`packages/hid/web/index.js:131`). The last `close` of a plugged HID device on Linux and Windows drops the open, so `inputReports` answers empty and interrupted and `hidConnection` says disconnected (`packages/hid/linux/lib.rs:328`, `windows/lib.rs:369`); Apple's first open retains its shared log and nothing releases it (`packages/hid/apple/HID.swift:204`).
  - Game. A sprite that fails `validate` journals the same refusal on every feed (`game/render/src/quads.rs:295`; both reviewers). `executionInputFiles` walks `assets/.DS_Store` into the proof inputs (`game/inputs.mjs:77`).
  - Documents and tests. `bench/extra-heavy/EXACT2-GAPS.md:124-126` describes `text` and `image` inside `svg` as refused; `text` compiles and `image` is refused with another message. On Linux, with and without the fixes: `js/tests/it/pure.rs:36` fails parsing Chrome's output, the runner's `every_app_plan_updates_incrementally_exactly_as_it_does_in_full` fails for `geofence-fixture`, `exact-textflow` fails at `src/tests.rs:75` and `src/showpiece_tests.rs:132`, `:326`, and `tamber_rows_check::a_worst_case_write_is_checked_within_the_budget` fails under load, a wall-clock budget (`docs/testing.md`).
- **A protected-routes recipe, written and not landed** (2026-11-11, #136; `wave14/route-guards` a737ec3b, `docs/recipes/protected-routes.md`). Three versions each passed their own tests on the web and macOS and then failed a case in review. Open on the last: a first session check that fails leaves a protected screen on its placeholder with no way to sign in (`known` is false, so nothing redirects and nothing refreshes `me`); a protected tab root is described in prose and not drawn by the snippet (`replace` at depth 1 keeps the tab's root route, so the redirect is refused and its one-shot timer is spent); signing in at `/login` with no `next` replaces the entry with a second `/` visit, and the browser's Back is then refused; the fragment of a deep link is lost across the redirect. The shape does not fit a recipe's 3,072 bytes. Two things make it intricate: nothing runs when a resource's answer arrives (RealWorld polls every 100 ms for it), and a `replace` and a `push` in one commit leave the browser's entry beneath with its old URL.
- **Landed on 2026-11-11 without a Windows machine or an Android device** (no Windows machine and no Zig for a cross-check; the Android emulator does not boot). Windows, each tested as a function of the platform on a Mac and never run there: the Android build's NDK tools (#148), a verbatim drive path as the filesystem helper's root (#149; its Windows test compiled, not run), starting Gradle with the wrapper jar and splitting `JAVA_OPTS` and `GRADLE_OPTS` as the C runtime does (#150).
